## Appendix: every captured page

Every Higgsfield page captured on 2026-10-16 from the site's sitemaps and navigation: 1060 pages. Screenshots were taken signed out at 1440×900 with the cookie notice hidden. Tool pages that need an account show their signed-out view.

| Group | Pages |
| --- | --- |
| Tools, studios and agent pages | 144 |
| Apps | 100 |
| Motion presets | 229 |
| Mixed media presets | 33 |
| Model and landing pages | 135 |
| Help center and changelog | 104 |
| Blog | 272 |
| Original series | 43 |
